It is a PIN, not a coin or a medal: the outline follows the emblem (a die-cut silhouette) and is a plain round disc only when the emblem itself is round; no border ring full of extra symbols, no second emblem, no background pattern. Badge centred, filling about 85% of the canvas, same camera, light and scale for the whole set. Isolated on a fully transparent background: no table, no backing card, no pin clasp visible, no cast shadow beyond a faint contact shadow. No text, no letters, no numbers, no watermark.
material: brushed stainless steel and signal-red hard enamel, with a few circuit lines engraved into the steel itself (no other colour)

description: WHAT: the title logo of the strategy game "2100 MEGANATIONS", shown in the main menu over changing pictures, so it must read on light and dark backgrounds. OBJECT: a long, low bar of smoked near-black glass (#060c10 to #14222b, slightly translucent at the edges, polished, a faint cold reflection) held by four small copper bolts. Mounted ON the bar, standing slightly proud of it: the word MEGANATIONS in tall, condensed art-deco capitals, each letter a thick piece of CAST COPPER (#c27a45, highlights #f0b585, shadows #6b3a1c), with real casting texture, edges worn bright by handling, a little green verdigris patina deep in the recesses, and a thin channel of glowing teal glass (#4fd6c8) running along the middle of each letter's main stroke like an inlay. At the LEFT end of the bar, overlapping it and a bit taller than the letters: the emblem of the mod, a round ring split into TEN equal segments, each segment a different coloured vitreous enamel set in copper cells (in order around the ring: old gold, chrome blue, signal red, sea teal, sun gold, jade green, ochre, imperial purple, crimson, wine red), and in the centre a dark glass disc with the number 2100 engraved in copper. The word must be spelled exactly M-E-G-A-N-A-T-I-O-N-S, nothing else written anywhere. Composition: the whole object fills the canvas width with a small margin, wider than tall.
